acn, writhe = self._writhe_and_crossing_numbers(\n                samples, recalculate=recalculate, **kwargs)\n\n        elif method == 'integral':\n            from pyknotid.spacecurves.complexity import writhe_and_acn_integral\n            writhe, acn = writhe_and_acn_integral(self.points, **kwargs)\n\n        if include_acn:\n            return writhe, acn\n        return writhe\n\n    def higher_order_writhe_integral(self.points, order=order,\n                                            try_cython=try_cython)\n\n    def second_order_writhes(self, try_cython=True, **kwargs):\n The crossings, and start point of the curve, can\n        optionally be marked.\n\n        The projection is drawn using matplotlib.\n\n        .. image:: example_knot_projection_9_5.png\n           :align: center\n           :scale: 50%\n           :alt: An example knot projection.\n\n        Parameters\n        ----------\n        with_crossings : bool\n            If True, marks the location of each self-intersection in\n            projection. Defaults to False.\n        rotate : bool\n            Whether to rotate the space curve before each run. Defaults\n            to True as this can make things much faster.\n        obey_knotting : bool\n            Whether to not let the line pass through itself. Defaults to\n            True as this is always what you want for a closed curve.\n        **kwargs\n            Any remaining kwargs are passed to the\n            :class:`pyknotid.simplify.octree.OctreeCell`\n            constructor.\n\n        '''\n        from pyknotid.simplify.octree import OctreeCell\n        for i in range(runs):\n            if len(self.points) > 30:\n                self._vprint('\\rRun {} of {}, {} points remain'.format(\n                    i, runs, len(self.points)))\n\n            if rotate:\n                rot_mat = get_rotation_matrix(n.random.random(3))\n                self._apply_matrix(rot_mat)\n\n            oc = OctreeCell.from_single_line(self.points, **kwargs)\n            oc.simplify(obey_knotting)\n            self._recent_octree = oc\n            self.points = oc.get_single_line()\n\n            if rotate:\n                self._apply_matrix(rot_mat.T)\n\n            if plot:\n                self.plot()\n\n        self._vprint('\\nReduced to {} points'.format(len(self.points)))\n\n    def arclength(self, include_closure=True):\n        '''\n        Returns the arclength of the line, the sum of lengths of each\n        piecewise linear segment.\n\n        Parameters\n        ----------\n        include_closure : bool\n            Whether to include the distance between the final and\n            first points. Defaults to True.\n        '''\n        return arclength(self.points, include_closure)\n\n    def radius_of_gyration(self):\n        '''\n        Returns the radius of gyration of the points of self,\n        assuming each has equal weight and ignoring the connecting\n        lines.\n        '''\n        return radius_of_gyration(self.points)\n\n    def radius_of_gyration_2(self):\n        rog_squared = 0.\n        for i, point in enumerate(self.points):\n            for next_point in self.points[i+1:]:\n                # next_point = self.points[i+1]\n                rog_squared += np.sum((next_point - point)**2)\n\n        rog_squared /= len(self.points)**2\n\n        return np.sqrt(rog_squared)\n            \n\n    def __len__(self):\n        return len(self.points)\n\n    def reparameterised(self, mode='arclength', num_points=None,\n                        interpolation='linear'):\n        '''\n        Returns a new :class:`SpaceCurve` where new points have been selected\n        by interpolating the current ones.\n\n        .. warning:: This doesn't do what you expect! The new segments\n                     will probably not all be separated by the right amount\n                     in terms of the new parameterisation.\n\n        Parameters\n        ----------\n        mode : str\n            The function to reparameterise by. Defaults to 'arclength',\n            which is currently the only option.\n        num_points : int\n            The number of points in the new parameterisation. Defaults\n            to None, which means the same as the current number.\n        interpolation : str\n            The type of interpolation to use, passed directly to the\n            ``kind`` option of ``scipy.interpolate.interp1d``. Defaults\n            to 'linear', and other options have not been tested.\n        '''\n        from scipy.interpolate import interp1d\n        indices = self._new_indices_by_arclength(num_points)\n\n        interp_xs = interp1d(range(len(self.points)+1),\n                             n.hstack((self.points[:, 0],\n                                      self.points[:, 0][:1])))\n        interp_ys = interp1d(range(len(self.points)+1),\n                             n.hstack((self.points[:, 1],\n                                      self.points[:, 1][:1])))\n        interp_zs = interp1d(range(len(self.points)+1),\n                             n.hstack((self.points[:, 2],\n                                      self.points[:, 2][:1])))\n\n        new_points = n.zeros((len(indices), 3), dtype=n.float)\n        new_points[:, 0] = interp_xs(indices)\n        new_points[:, 1] = interp_ys(indices)\n        new_points[:, 2] = interp_zs(indices)\n\n        return SpaceCurve(new_points)\n\n    def _new_indices_by_arclength(self, number, step=None, gap=0):\n        if number is None:\n            number = len(self.points)\n        total_arclength = self.arclength()\n        if step is None:\n            arclengths = n.linspace(0, total_arclength - gap, number+1)[:-1]\n        else:\n            arclengths = n.arange(0, total_arclength - gap, step)\n\n        arclengths[0] += 0.000001\n\n        points = self.points\n        segment_arclengths = self.segment_arclengths()\n        cumulative_arclength = n.hstack([[0.], n.cumsum(segment_arclengths)])\n        total_arclength = self.arclength()\n\n        indices = []\n\n        for arclength in arclengths:\n            first_greater_index = n.argmax(cumulative_arclength > arclength)\n            last_lower_index = (first_greater_index - 1) % len(points)\n            arclength_below = cumulative_arclength[last_lower_index]\n            step_arclength = segment_arclengths[last_lower_index]\n            step_fraction = (arclength - arclength_below) / step_arclength\n            indices.append(last_lower_index + step_fraction)\n\n        return indices\n\n    def segment_arclengths(self):\n        '''\n        Returns an array of arclengths of every step in the line\n        defined by self.points.\n        '''\n        return n.apply_along_axis(\n            mag, 1, n.roll(self.points, -1, axis=0) - self.points)\n\n    def smooth(self, repeats=1, periodic=True, window_len=10,\n               window='hanning'):\n        '''Smooths each of the x, y and z components of self.points by\n        convolving with a window of the given type and size.\n\n        .. warning:: This is *not* topologically safe, it can change\n                     the knot type of the curve. For topologically\n                     safe reduction, see :meth:`octree_simplify`.\n\n        Parameters\n        ----------\n        repeats : int\n            Number of times to run the smoothing algorithm. Defaults to 1.\n        periodic : bool\n            If True, the convolution window wraps around the curve.\n            Defaults to True.\n        window_len : int\n            Width of the convolution window. Defaults to 10.\n            Passed to :func:`pyknotid.spacecurves.smooth.smooth`.\n        window : string\n            The type of convolution window. Defaults to 'hanning'.\n            Passed to :func:`pyknotid.spacecurves.smooth.smooth`.\n        '''\n        points = self.points\n        if periodic:\n            points = n.vstack((points[-(window_len + 1):],\n                               points,\n                               points[:(window_len + 1)]))\n        else:\n            start = points[0]\n            end = points[-1]\n        if len(points) > window_len:\n            for i in range(repeats):\n                points[:, 0] = smooth(points[:, 0], window_len, window)\n                points[:, 1] = smooth(points[:, 1], window_len, window)\n                points[:, 2] = smooth(points[:, 2], window_len, window)\n                if not periodic:\n                    points[0] = start\n                    points[-1] = end\n        if periodic:\n            points = points[(window_len + 1):-(window_len + 1)]\n        self.points = points\n\n    def simplify_straight_segments(self, closed=False):\n        '''Replaces successive curve segments with identical tangents with a\n        single longer segment.'''\n\n        points = self.points\n        ts = np.roll(points, -1, axis=0) - points\n\n        keep_points = np.ones(len(points), dtype=np.bool)\n\n        ts_range = ts[:-2] if not closed else ts\n\n        for i1, t1 in enumerate(ts_range):\n            t1 /= np.sqrt(np.sum(t1**2))\n            t2 = ts[(i1 + 1) % len(ts)]\n            t2 /= np.sqrt(np.sum(t2**2))\n            if np.dot(t1, t2) > (1 - 10e-10):\n                keep_points[i1 + 1] = 0\n\n        self.points = self.points[keep_points]\n\n    def curvatures(self, closed=True):\n        '''Returns curvatures at each vertex (or really line segment).'''\n        points = self.points\n\n        ts = np.roll(points, -1, axis=0) - points\n        vs = np.sqrt(np.sum(ts*ts, axis=1))\n\n        gamma_dd = 0.25 * (np.roll(points, -2, axis=0) + np.roll(points, 2, axis=0) - 2*points)\n        vns = 0.5 * (vs + np.roll(vs, 1))\n        gamma_d = 0.5 * (np.roll(points, -1, axis=0) - np.roll(points, 1, axis=0))\n        ans = 0.25 * (np.roll(vs, -1) + vs - np.roll(vs, 1) - np.roll(vs, 2))\n        kappas = gamma_dd - np.einsum('i,ij->ij', (ans / vns), gamma_d)\n        kappas = np.sqrt(np.sum(kappas * kappas, axis=1))\n        kappas = (1./vns**2) * kappas\n\n        if not closed:\n            kappas[:3] = kappas[3]\n            kappas[-3:] = kappas[-4]\n\n        return kappas\n\n    def sum_of_angles(self):\n        points = self.points\n        segments = np.roll(points, -1, axis=0) - points\n        segments_2 = np.roll(segments, -1, axis=0)\n\n        mags = np.sqrt(np.sum(segments * segments, axis=1))\n        mags_2 = np.roll(mags, -1)\n\n        angles = np.arccos(np.sum(segments * segments_2, axis=1) / (mags * mags_2))\n        return np.sum(angles)\n\n\n    def torsions(self, signed=False, closed=True):\n        '''Returns torsions at each vertex.'''\n\n        d = self.points\n\n        ts = np.roll(d, -1, axis=0) - d\n        vs = np.sqrt(np.sum(ts*ts, axis=1))\n\n        gamma_dd = 0.25 * (np.roll(d, -2, axis=0) + np.roll(d, 2, axis=0) - 2*d)\n        vns = 0.5 * (vs + np.roll(vs, 1))\n        gamma_d = 0.5 * (np.roll(d, -1, axis=0) - np.roll(d, 1, axis=0))\n        ans = 0.25 * (np.roll(vs, -1) + vs - np.roll(vs, 1) - np.roll(vs, 2))\n\n        kappas = gamma_dd - np.einsum('i,ij->ij', (ans / vns), gamma_d)\n        kappas = np.sum(kappas * kappas, axis=1)\n\n        gamma_ddd = 0.125 * (np.roll(d, -3, axis=0) - 3*np.roll(d, -1, axis=0) +\n                            3*np.roll(d, 1, axis=0) - np.roll(d, 3, axis=0))\n\n        numerator = np.sum(np.cross(gamma_d, gamma_dd) * gamma_ddd, axis=1)\n        denominator = vns**2 * np.abs(kappas)\n\n        torsions = numerator / denominator\n        if not signed:\n            torsions = np.abs(torsions)\n\n        if not closed:\n            torsions[:3] = torsions[3]\n            torsions[-3:] = torsions[-4]\n\n        return torsions\n\n    def close(self):\n        '''Adds the starting point to the end of the curve, so that it ends\n        exactly where it began.\n\n        '''\n        self.points = np.vstack([self.points, self.points[:1]])\n\n    def interpolate(self, num_points, s=0, **kwargs):\n        '''Replaces self.points with points from a B-spline interpolation.\n\n        This method uses scipy.interpolate.splprep. kwargs are passed\n        to this function.\n        '''\n        from scipy.interpolate import splprep, splev\n\n        lengths = self.segment_arclengths()\n        cum_length_fracs = np.cumsum(lengths) / np.sum(lengths)\n\n        tck, u = splprep([self.points[:, 0],\n                          self.points[:, 1],\n                          self.points[:, 2]],\n                         u=cum_length_fracs,\n                         **kwargs)\n        points = splev(np.linspace(0, 1, When this occurs,\n        subtracts the lattice vector in this direction.\n\n        Parameters\n        ----------\n        shape : array-like\n            The x, y, z distances of the periodic      rest = points[i:]\n            change = curLine - prevLine\n            if -1.05*dx < change[0] < -0.95*dx:\n                rest[:, 0] += dx\n            if 1.05*dx > change[0] > 0.95*dx:\n                rest[:, 0] -= dx\n            if -1.05*dy < change[1] < -0.95*dy:\n                rest[:, 1] += dy\n            if 1.05*dy > change[1] > 0.95*dy:\n                rest[:, 1] -= dy\n @classmethod\n    def from_gauss_code(cls, code):\n        '''Creates a Knot from the given code, which must be provided as a\n        string and may optionally include crossing orientations (these are\n        actually ignored).'''\n        from pyknotid.representations import Representation\n        if 'a' in code or 'c' in code:\n            k = cls(Representation(code).space_curve())\n    line : ndarray\n            The points of the line.\n        com : iterable\n            Optional additional centre of mass to shift by before closing\n\n        '''\n        com = '''Returns a :class:`SpaceCurve` instance in which the line has been\n        slightly translated and rotated, in order to (practically) ensure\n        no self intersections in closure or coincident If None, random\n            angles are used. Defaults to None.\n        '''\n        if angles is None:\n            angles = n.random.random(3)\n        phi, theta, psi = angles\n        rot_mat = get_rotation_matrix(angles)\n        self._apply_matrix(rot_mat)\n\n    def _apply_matrix(self, mat):\n        '''\n        Applies the given matrix to all of self.points.\n        '''\n        self.points = n.apply_along_axis(mat.dot, 1, self.points)\n\n    def cuaps(self, include_closure=True):\n        '''Returns a list of the 'cuaps', where the curve is parallel to the\n        positive x-axis. See D Bar-Natan and R van der Veen, \"A\n        polynomial time knot polynomial\", 2017.\n        '''\n        points = self.points\n        if mag(points[-1] - points[0]) < 0.000001:\n            points = points[:-1]  # Avoids a badly defined tangent\n        tangents = np.roll(points, -1, axis=0) - points\n        angles = np.arctan2(tangents[:, 1], tangents[:, 0])\n\n        print('angles', angles)\n\n        cuaps = []\n\n        for i in range(len(points) - 2):\n            angle = angles[i]\n            next_angle = angles[i + 1]\n\n            if (0 < angle < np.pi / 2.) and (-np.pi / 2. < next_angle < 0):\n                cuaps.append([i + 1, -1])\n            elif (-np.pi / 2. < angle < 0) and (0 < next_angle < np.pi / 2.):\n                cuaps.append([i + 1, 1])\n\n        if include_closure:\n            angle = angles[-2]\n            next_angle = angles[-1]\n            if (0 < angle < np.pi / 2.) and (-np.pi / 2. < next_angle < 0):\n                cuaps.append([len(angles) - 1, -1])\n            elif (-np.pi / 2. < angle < 0) and (0 < next_angle < np.pi / 2.):\n                cuaps.append([len(angles) - 1, 1])\n\n            angle = angles[-1]\n            next_angle = angles[0]\n            if (0 < angle < np.pi / 2.) and (-np.pi / 2. < next_angle < 0):\n                cuaps.append([0, -1])\n            elif (-np.pi / 2. < angle < 0) and (0 < next_angle < np.pi / 2.):\n                cuaps.append([0, 1])\n\n        cuaps = np.array(cuaps)\n        # cuaps[:, -1] *= -1\n\n        return cuaps\n\n    def raw_crossings(self, mode='use_max_jump', include_closure=True,\n                      recalculate=False, try_cython=True):\n\n        '''Returns the crossings in the diagram of the projection of the\n        space curve into its z=0 plane.\n\n        The crossings will be calculated the first time this function\n        is called, then cached until an operation that would change\n        the list (e.g. rotation, or changing ``self.points``).\n\n        Multiple modes are available (see parameters) - you should be\n        aware of this because different modes may be vastly slower or\n        faster depending on the type of line.\n\n        Parameters\n        ----------\n        mode : str, optional\n            One of ``'count_every_jump'`` or ``'use_max_jump'`` or\n            ``'naive'``. In the first case,\n            walking along the line uses information about the length of\n            every step. In the second, it guesses that all steps have the\n            same length as the maximum step length. In the last, no\n            optimisation is made and every crossing is checked.\n            The optimal choice depends on the data but is usually\n            ``'use_max_jump'``, which is the default.\n        include_closure : bool, optional\n            Whether to include crossings with the\n            line joining the start and end points. Defaults to True.\n        recalculate : bool, optional\n            Whether to force a recalculation of the crossing positions.\n            Defaults to False.\n        try_cython : bool, optional\n            Whether to force the use of the python (as opposed to cython)\n            implementation of find_crossings. This will make no difference\n            if the cython could not be loaded, in which case python is already\n            used automatically. Defaults to True.\n\n        Returns\n        -------\n        array-like\n            The raw array of floats representing crossings, of the\n            form [[line_index, other_index, +-1, +-1], ...], where the\n            line_index and other_index are in arclength parameterised\n            by integers for each vertex and linearly interpolated,\n            and the +-1 represent over/under and clockwise/anticlockwise\n            respectively.\n        '''\n\n        if not recalculate and self._crossings is not None:\n            return self._crossings\n\n        if try_cython:\n            helpers_module = chelpers\n        else:\n            helpers_module = helpers\n\n        self._vprint('Finding crossings')\n\n        points = self.points\n        segment_lengths = n.roll(points[:, :2], -1, axis=0) - points[:, :2]\n        segment_lengths = n.sqrt(n.sum(segment_lengths * segment_lengths,\n                                       axis=1))\n        # if include_closure:\n        #     max_segment_length = n.max(segment_lengths)\n        # else:\n        max_segment_length = n.max(segment_lengths[:-1])\n\n        numtries = len(points) - 3\n\n        crossings = []\n\n        jump_mode = {'count_every_jump': 1, 'use_max_jump': 2,\n                     'naive': 3}[mode]\n\n        for i in range(len(points)-2):\n            if self.verbose:\n                if i % 100 == 0:\n                    self._vprint('\\ri = {} / {}'.format(i, numtries),\n                                 False)\n            v0 = points[i]\n            dv = points[(i+1) % len(points)] - v0\n\n            s = points[i+2:]\n            vnum = i\n            compnum = i+2\n\n            crossings.extend(helpers_module.find_crossings(\n                v0, dv, s, segment_lengths[compnum:],\n                vnum, compnum,\n                max_segment_length,\n                jump_mode\n                ))\n\n        if include_closure:\n            closure_segment_length = segment_lengths[-1]\n            max_segment_length = n.max([closure_segment_length,\n                                        max_segment_length])\n            v0 = points[-1]\n            dv = points[0] - v0\n            s = points[1:-1]\n            vnum = len(points) - 1\n            compnum = 1\n            crossings.extend(helpers_module.find_crossings(\n                v0, dv, s, segment_lengths[compnum:],\n                vnum, compnum,\n                max_segment_length,\n                jump_mode))\n\n        self._vprint('\\n{} crossings found\\n'.format(len(crossings) / 2))\n        crossings.sort(key=lambda s: s[0])\n        crossings = n.array(crossings)\n        self._crossings = crossings\n\n        return crossings\n\n    def planar_writhe(self, **kwargs):\n        '''\n        Returns the current planar writhe of the knot; the signed sum\n        of crossings of the current projection.\n\n        The 'true' writhe is the average of this quantity over all\n        projection directions, and is available from the :meth:`writhe`\n        method.\n\n        Parameters\n        ----------\n        **kwargs :\n            These are passed directly to :meth:`raw_crossings`.\n        '''\n        crossings = self.raw_crossings(**kwargs)\n        return n.sum(crossings[:, 3]) / 2.\n\n    def distance_quantity(self):\n        from pyknotid.spacecurves.complexity import distance_quantity\n        return distance_quantity(self.points)\n\n    def writhe(self, samples=10, recalculate=False, method='integral',\n               include_acn=False, **kwargs):\n        '''\n        The (approximate) writhe of the space curve, obtained by averaging\n        the planar writhe over the given number of directions.\n\n        Parameters\n        ----------\n        samples : int\n            The number of directions to average over. Defaults to 10.\n        recalculate : bool\n            Whether to recalculate the writhe even if a cached result\n            is available. If 'integral', calculates the writhing integral.\n        **kwargs :\n            These are passed directly to :meth:`raw_crossings`.\n        '''\n        if method == 'projections':\n            acn, writhe = self._writhe_and_crossing_numbers(\n                samples, recalculate=recalculate, **kwargs)\n\n        elif method == 'integral':\n            from pyknotid.spacecurves.complexity import writhe_and_acn_integral\n            writhe, acn = writhe_and_acn_integral(self.points, **kwargs)\n\n        if include_acn:\n            return writhe, acn\n        return writhe\n\n    def higher_order_writhe_integral(self.points, order=order,\n                                            try_cython=try_cython)\n\n    def second_order_writhes(self, try_cython=True, **kwargs):\n        from pyknotid.spacecurves.complexity import second_order_writhes\n        return second_order_writhes(self.points, try_cython=try_cython, **kwargs)\n\n    def planar_second_order_writhe(self, **kwargs):\n        '''The second order writhe (type 2, i1,i3,i2,i4) of the projection of\n        the curve along the z axis.\n        '''\n        from pyknotid.invariants import def gauss_code(self, recalculate=False, **kwargs):\n        '''\n        Returns a :class:`~pyknotid.representations.gausscode.GaussCode`\n        instance representing the crossings of the knot.\n\n The crossings, and start point of the curve, can\n        optionally be marked.\n\n        The projection is drawn using matplotlib.\n\n        .. image:: example_knot_projection_9_5.png\n           :align: center\n           :scale: 50%\n           :alt: An example knot projection.\n\n        Parameters\n        ----------\n        with_crossings : bool\n            If True, marks the location of each self-intersection in\n            projection. Default to False.\n        fig_ax : tuple\n            A 2-tuple of the matplotlib (fig, ax) to use, or None\n            to create a new pair.\n        show : bool\n            If True, opens a new window showing the drawing. Defaults\n            to True.\n\n        Returns\n        -------\n        A 2-tuple of the matplotlib (fig, ax) used for the drawing.\n        '''\n        points = self.points\n        crossings = None\n        plot_crossings = []\n        if with_crossings:\n            crossings = self.raw_crossings()\n            plot_crossings = []\n            for crossing in crossings:\n                x, y, over, orientation = crossing\n                xint = int(x)\n                r = points[xint]\n                dr = points[(xint+1) % len(points)] - r\n                plot_crossings.append(r + (x-xint) * dr)\n        fig, ax = plot_projection(points,\n                                  crossings=n.array(plot_crossings),\n                                  mark_start=mark_start,\n                                  mark_points=mark_points,\n                                  fig_ax=fig_ax,\n                                  show=show)\n        return fig, ax\n\n    def __str__(self):\n        if self._crossings is not None:\n            return '<SpaceCurve with {} points, {} crossings>'.format(\n                len(self.points), len(self._crossings))\n        return '<SpaceCurve with {} points>'.format(len(self.points))\n\n    def __repr__(self):\n        return str(self)\n\n    def to_json(self, filen):\n        '''\n        Writes the knot points to the given filename, in a json format\n        that can be read later by :meth:`SpaceCurve.from_json`. Defaults to False.\n        rotate : bool\n            Whether to rotate the space curve before each run. Defaults\n            to True as this can make things much faster.\n        obey_knotting : bool\n            Whether to not let the line pass through itself. Defaults to\n            True as this is always what you want for a closed curve.\n        **kwargs\n            Any remaining kwargs are passed to the\n            :class:`pyknotid.simplify.octree.OctreeCell`\n            constructor.\n\n        '''\n        from pyknotid.simplify.octree import OctreeCell\n        for i in range(runs):\n            if len(self.points) > 30:\n                self._vprint('\\rRun {} of {}, {} points remain'.format(\n                    i, runs, len(self.points)))\n\n            if rotate:\n                rot_mat = get_rotation_matrix(n.random.random(3))\n                self._apply_matrix(rot_mat)\n\n            oc = OctreeCell.from_single_line(self.points, **kwargs)\n            oc.simplify(obey_knotting)\n            self._recent_octree = oc\n            self.points = oc.get_single_line()\n\n            if rotate:\n                self._apply_matrix(rot_mat.T)\n\n            if plot:\n                self.plot()\n\n        self._vprint('\\nReduced to {} points'.format(len(self.points)))\n\n    def arclength(self, include_closure=True):\n        '''\n        Returns the arclength of the line, the sum of lengths of each\n        piecewise linear segment.\n\n        Parameters\n        ----------\n        include_closure : bool\n            Whether to include the distance between the final and\n            first points. Defaults to True.\n        '''\n        return arclength(self.points, include_closure)\n\n    def radius_of_gyration(self):\n        '''\n        Returns the radius of gyration of the points of self,\n        assuming each has equal weight and ignoring the connecting\n        lines.\n        '''\n        return radius_of_gyration(self.points)\n\n    def radius_of_gyration_2(self):\n        rog_squared = 0.\n        for i, point in enumerate(self.points):\n            for next_point in self.points[i+1:]:\n                # next_point = self.points[i+1]\n                rog_squared += np.sum((next_point - point)**2)\n\n        rog_squared /= len(self.points)**2\n\n        return np.sqrt(rog_squared)\n            \n\n    def __len__(self):\n        return len(self.points)\n\n    def reparameterised(self, mode='arclength', num_points=None,\n                        interpolation='linear'):\n        '''\n        Returns a new :class:`SpaceCurve` where new points have been selected\n        by interpolating the current ones.\n\n        .. warning:: This doesn't do what you expect! The new segments\n                     will probably not all be separated by the right amount\n                     in terms of the new parameterisation.\n\n        Parameters\n        ----------\n        mode : str\n            The function to reparameterise by. Defaults to 'arclength',\n            which is currently the only option.\n        num_points : int\n            The number of points in the new parameterisation. Defaults\n            to None, which means the same as the current number.\n        interpolation : str\n            The type of interpolation to use, passed directly to the\n            ``kind`` option of ``scipy.interpolate.interp1d``. Defaults\n            to 'linear', and other options have not been tested.\n        '''\n        from scipy.interpolate import interp1d\n        indices = self._new_indices_by_arclength(num_points)\n\n        interp_xs = interp1d(range(len(self.points)+1),\n                             n.hstack((self.points[:, 0],\n                                      self.points[:, 0][:1])))\n        interp_ys = interp1d(range(len(self.points)+1),\n                             n.hstack((self.points[:, 1],\n                                      self.points[:, 1][:1])))\n        interp_zs = interp1d(range(len(self.points)+1),\n                             n.hstack((self.points[:, 2],\n                                      self.points[:, 2][:1])))\n\n        new_points = n.zeros((len(indices), 3), dtype=n.float64)\n        new_points[:, 0] = interp_xs(indices)\n        new_points[:, 1] = interp_ys(indices)\n        new_points[:, 2] = interp_zs(indices)\n\n        return SpaceCurve(new_points)\n\n    def _new_indices_by_arclength(self, number, step=None, gap=0):\n        if number is None:\n            number = len(self.points)\n        total_arclength = self.arclength()\n        if step is None:\n            arclengths = n.linspace(0, total_arclength - gap, number+1)[:-1]\n        else:\n            arclengths = n.arange(0, total_arclength - gap, step)\n\n        arclengths[0] += 0.000001\n\n        points = self.points\n        segment_arclengths = self.segment_arclengths()\n        cumulative_arclength = n.hstack([[0.], n.cumsum(segment_arclengths)])\n        total_arclength = self.arclength()\n\n        indices = []\n\n        for arclength in arclengths:\n            first_greater_index = n.argmax(cumulative_arclength > arclength)\n            last_lower_index = (first_greater_index - 1) % len(points)\n            arclength_below = cumulative_arclength[last_lower_index]\n            step_arclength = segment_arclengths[last_lower_index]\n            step_fraction = (arclength - arclength_below) / step_arclength\n            indices.append(last_lower_index + step_fraction)\n\n        return indices\n\n    def segment_arclengths(self):\n        '''\n        Returns an array of arclengths of every step in the line\n        defined by self.points.\n        '''\n        return n.apply_along_axis(\n            mag, 1, n.roll(self.points, -1, axis=0) - self.points)\n\n    def smooth(self, repeats=1, periodic=True, window_len=10,\n               window='hanning'):\n        '''Smooths each of the x, y and z components of self.points by\n        convolving with a window of the given type and size.\n\n        .. warning:: This is *not* topologically safe, it can change\n                     the knot type of the curve. For topologically\n                     safe reduction, see :meth:`octree_simplify`.\n\n        Parameters\n        ----------\n        repeats : int\n            Number of times to run the smoothing algorithm. Defaults to 1.\n        periodic : bool\n            If True, the convolution window wraps around the curve.\n            Defaults to True.\n        window_len : int\n            Width of the convolution window. Defaults to 10.\n            Passed to :func:`pyknotid.spacecurves.smooth.smooth`.\n        window : string\n            The type of convolution window. Defaults to 'hanning'.\n            Passed to :func:`pyknotid.spacecurves.smooth.smooth`.\n        '''\n        points = self.points\n        if periodic:\n            points = n.vstack((points[-(window_len + 1):],\n                               points,\n                               points[:(window_len + 1)]))\n        else:\n            start = points[0]\n            end = points[-1]\n        if len(points) > window_len:\n            for i in range(repeats):\n                points[:, 0] = smooth(points[:, 0], window_len, window)\n                points[:, 1] = smooth(points[:, 1], window_len, window)\n                points[:, 2] = smooth(points[:, 2], window_len, window)\n                if not periodic:\n                    points[0] = start\n                    points[-1] = end\n        if periodic:\n            points = points[(window_len + 1):-(window_len + 1)]\n        self.points = points\n\n    def simplify_straight_segments(self, closed=False):\n        '''Replaces successive curve segments with identical tangents with a\n        single longer segment.'''\n\n        points = self.points\n        ts = np.roll(points, -1, axis=0) - points\n\n        keep_points = np.ones(len(points), dtype=np.bool)\n\n        ts_range = ts[:-2] if not closed else ts\n\n        for i1, t1 in enumerate(ts_range):\n            t1 /= np.sqrt(np.sum(t1**2))\n            t2 = ts[(i1 + 1) % len(ts)]\n            t2 /= np.sqrt(np.sum(t2**2))\n            if np.dot(t1, t2) > (1 - 10e-10):\n                keep_points[i1 + 1] = 0\n\n        self.points = self.points[keep_points]\n\n    def curvatures(self, closed=True):\n        '''Returns curvatures at each vertex (or really line segment).'''\n        points = self.points\n\n        ts = np.roll(points, -1, axis=0) - points\n        vs = np.sqrt(np.sum(ts*ts, axis=1))\n\n        gamma_dd = 0.25 * (np.roll(points, -2, axis=0) + np.roll(points, 2, axis=0) - 2*points)\n        vns = 0.5 * (vs + np.roll(vs, 1))\n        gamma_d = 0.5 * (np.roll(points, -1, axis=0) - np.roll(points, 1, axis=0))\n        ans = 0.25 * (np.roll(vs, -1) + vs - np.roll(vs, 1) - np.roll(vs, 2))\n        kappas = gamma_dd - np.einsum('i,ij->ij', (ans / vns), gamma_d)\n        kappas = np.sqrt(np.sum(kappas * kappas, axis=1))\n        kappas = (1./vns**2) * kappas\n\n        if not closed:\n            kappas[:3] = kappas[3]\n            kappas[-3:] = kappas[-4]\n\n        return kappas\n\n    def sum_of_angles(self):\n        points = self.points\n        segments = np.roll(points, -1, axis=0) - points\n        segments_2 = np.roll(segments, -1, axis=0)\n\n        mags = np.sqrt(np.sum(segments * segments, axis=1))\n        mags_2 = np.roll(mags, -1)\n\n        angles = np.arccos(np.sum(segments * segments_2, axis=1) / (mags * mags_2))\n        return np.sum(angles)\n\n\n    def torsions(self, signed=False, closed=True):\n        '''Returns torsions at each vertex.'''\n\n        d = self.points\n\n        ts = np.roll(d, -1, axis=0) - d\n        vs = np.sqrt(np.sum(ts*ts, axis=1))\n\n        gamma_dd = 0.25 * (np.roll(d, -2, axis=0) + np.roll(d, 2, axis=0) - 2*d)\n        vns = 0.5 * (vs + np.roll(vs, 1))\n        gamma_d = 0.5 * (np.roll(d, -1, axis=0) - np.roll(d, 1, axis=0))\n        ans = 0.25 * (np.roll(vs, -1) + vs - np.roll(vs, 1) - np.roll(vs, 2))\n\n        kappas = gamma_dd - np.einsum('i,ij->ij', (ans / vns), gamma_d)\n        kappas = np.sum(kappas * kappas, axis=1)\n\n        gamma_ddd = 0.125 * (np.roll(d, -3, axis=0) - 3*np.roll(d, -1, axis=0) +\n                            3*np.roll(d, 1, axis=0) - np.roll(d, 3, axis=0))\n\n        numerator = np.sum(np.cross(gamma_d, gamma_dd) * gamma_ddd, axis=1)\n        denominator = vns**2 * np.abs(kappas)\n\n        torsions = numerator / denominator\n        if not signed:\n            torsions = np.abs(torsions)\n\n        if not closed:\n            torsions[:3] = torsions[3]\n            torsions[-3:] = torsions[-4]\n\n        return torsions\n\n    def close(self):\n        '''Adds the starting point to the end of the curve, so that it ends\n        exactly where it began.\n\n        '''\n        self.points = np.vstack([self.points, self.points[:1]])\n\n    def interpolate(self, num_points, s=0, **kwargs):\n        '''Replaces self.points with points from a B-spline interpolation.\n\n        This method uses scipy.interpolate.splprep. kwargs are passed\n        to this function.\n        '''\n        from scipy.interpolate import splprep, splev\n\n        lengths = self.segment_arclengths()\n        cum_length_fracs = np.cumsum(lengths) / np.sum(lengths)\n\n        tck, u = splprep([self.points[:, 0],\n                          self.points[:, 1],\n                          self.points[:, 2]],\n                         u=cum_length_fracs,\n                         **kwargs)\n        points = splev(np.linspace(0, 1, num_points),\n                            tck)\n\n        new_points = np.zeros((num_points, 3))\n        new_points[:, 0] = points[0]\n        new_points[:, 1] = points[1]\n        new_points[:, 2] = points[2]\n\n        self.points = new_points\n\n\n\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":1080,"oldLines":7,"newStart":1080,"newLines":7,"lines":["         points = self.points","         ts = np.roll(points, -1, axis=0) - crossings_connectedness_order(rep)\n    if len(crossings) < 2:\n        return 1\n    \n    web = []\n    crossing = crossings[0]\n    crossing = PlanarDiagram(crossing)\n    diag_1, diag_2 = kauffman_skein(crossing)\n    web.append([diag_1, A])\n    web.append([diag_2, 1.0/A])\n    replacement = -A**2 - A**-2\n\n    for crossing in crossings[1:]:\n        crossing = PlanarDiagram(crossing)\n        diag_1, diag_2 = kauffman_skein(crossing)\n        new_web = []\n\n        for term in web:\n            diag_1_product = PlanarDiagram(\n                [x for x in diag_1] +\n                [x for x in term[0]]).contract_points()\n            diag_2_product = PlanarDiagram(\n                [x for x in diag_2] +\n                [x for x in term[0]]).contract_points()\n            contracted_1 = 0\n            contracted_2 = 0\n            diag_1_new = []\n            diag_2_new = []\n\n            for point in diag_1_product:\n                if len(point.components()) == 1:\n                    contracted_1 += 1\n                else:\n                    diag_1_new.append(point)\n            for point in diag_2_product:\n                if len(point.components()) == 1:\n                    contracted_2 += 1\n                else:\n                    diag_2_new.append(point)\n\n            new_term_1 = ([sorted(diag_1_new)] +\n                          [term[1] * (replacement ** contracted_1) * A])\n            new_term_2 = ([sorted(diag_2_new)] +\n                          [term[1] * (replacement ** contracted_2) * 1.0/A])\n\n            new_web.append(new_term_1)\n            new_web.append(new_term_2)\n\n        unique_diags = list(set([tuple(x[0]) for x in new_web]))\n        contracted_web = [[list(x)] for x in unique_diags]\n\n        for term in new_web:\n            for diag in contracted_web:\n                if diag[0] == term[0]:\n                    if len(diag) == 1:\n                        diag.append(term[1])\n                    else:\n                        diag[1] += term[1]\n\n        web = contracted_web\n\n    # quick and dirty writhe calculation\n    crossings = [x for x in rep if isinstance(x, Crossing)]\n    total_writhe = 0\n    for crossing in crossings:\n        total_writhe += crossing.sign()\n        \n    jones_precursor = web[0][1]\n\n    jones_poly = (-A**3)**total_writhe * jones_precursor / replacement\n\n    if isinstance(var, sym.Symbol):\n        return sym.expand(sym.simplify(jones_poly)).subs(A, var**0.25)\n    return round(abs(jones_poly))\n\n\ndef crossings_connectedness_order(planar_diagram):\n    from pyknotid.representations.planardiagram import Crossing\n    crossings = [x for x in planar_diagram if isinstance(x, Crossing)]\n\n    inner_components = crossings[0].components()\n    crossings_remaining = crossings[1:]\n    ordered_crossings = [crossings[0]]\n\n    for i in range(len(crossings_remaining)):\n        connectedness = []\n        for crossing in crossings_remaining:\n            new_components = (len(list(set(crossing.components() + inner_components))) -\n                              len(inner_components))\n\n            connectedness.append(len(crossing.components()) - new_components)\n        most_connected_index = connectedness.index(max(connectedness))\n        inner_components += crossings_remaining[most_connected_index].components()\n        inner_components = list(set(inner_components))\n        ordered_crossings.append(crossings_remaining[most_connected_index])\n        del crossings_remaining[most_connected_index]\n\n    return ordered_crossings\n\ndef contract_points(planar_diagram):\n    '''\n    For appropriately contracting :class: `Points` in a\n    :class: `PlanarDiagram` According to the following rules:\n\n    P_a,b P_b,c -> P_a,c\n    P_a,b P_a,b -> P_a,a\n    '''\n    import copy\n\n    pd_crossings = [x for x in planar_diagram if type(x) == Crossing]\n    pd_points = [x for x in planar_diagram if type(x) == Point]\n\n    pd_trimmed = PlanarDiagram()\n    for crossing in pd_crossings:\n        pd_trimmed.append(crossing)\n\n    substituted_points = []\n    for i in range(len(pd_points)):\n        if pd_points[i] not in substituted_points:\n            pd_trimmed.append(pd_points[i])\n            totally_simplified = False\n            while not totally_simplified:\n                pd_trimmed_old = copy.copy(pd_trimmed)\n                for j in range(i+1, len(pd_points)):\n                    if (len(pd_trimmed[-1].components()) > 1 and\n                            len(pd_points[j].components()) > 1 and\n                            pd_points[j] not in substituted_points):\n                        # This skips P_a,a situations\n                        if pd_trimmed[-1].components() != pd_points[j].components():\n                            # This conditions skips (P_a,b P_a,b) situations\n                            substituted = False\n                            p = 0\n                            while substituted is not True and p != 2:\n                                q = 0\n                                while substituted is not True and q != 2:\n                                    if pd_trimmed[-1].components()[p] == pd_points[j].components()[q]:\n                                        pd_trimmed.append(Point(pd_trimmed[-1].components()[(p+1) % 2],\n                                                                pd_points[j].components()[(q+1) % 2]))\n                                        del pd_trimmed[-2]\n                                        substituted = True\n                                        substituted_points.append(pd_points[j])\n                                    q += 1\n                                p += 1\n                        else:\n                            # This deals with (P_a,b P_a,b) situations\n                            pd_trimmed.append(Point(pd_trimmed[-1].components()[0],\n                                                    pd_trimmed[-1].components()[0]))\n                            del pd_trimmed[-2]\n                            substituted_points.append(pd_points[j])\n                if pd_trimmed_old == pd_trimmed:\n                    totally_simplified = True\n    return pd_trimmed\n\n\ndef _write_mathematica_script(filen, text):\n    '''\n    Write the given text (mathematica code) to the given Gauss diagram) and signs, both in dictionaries.'''\n\n    ## Arrow diagram for v_2 is\n    ## appear -> leave -> leave -> appear (with crossed arrows)\n    over_crossing_indices = {}\n    under_crossing_indices = {}\n    signs = {}\n    for i, row in enumerate(gc):\n        if row[1] == 1:\n            over_crossing_indices[row[0]] = i\n        else:\n            under_crossing_indices[row[0]] = i\n        signs[row[0]] = row[2]\n\n    arrows = {index: (over_crossing_indices[index],\n                      under_crossing_indices[index])\n              for index in crossing_numbers}\n\n    return arrows, signs\n\ndef _crossing_arrows_and_signs_numpy(gc, crossing_numbers):\n    ## Arrow diagram for v_2 is\n    ## appear -> leave -> leave -> appear (with crossed arrows)\n    over_crossing_indices = {}\n    under_crossing_indices = {}\n    signs = {}\n    for i, row in enumerate(gc):\n        if row[1] == 1:\n            over_crossing_indices[row[0]] = i\n        else:\n            under_crossing_indices[row[0]] = i\n        signs[row[0]] = row[2]\n\n    arrows = n.zeros((len(crossing_numbers), 3), dtype=n.long)\n\n    for index, number in enumerate(crossing_numbers):\n        row = arrows[index]\n        row[0] = over_crossing_indices[number]\n        row[1] = under_crossing_indices[number]\n        row[2] = signs[number]\n\n    return arrows\n\ndef second_order_writhe(representation):\n    '''Returns the second order writhe (i1,i3,i2,i4) of the\n    representation, as defined in Lin and Wang.\n    '''\n\n    from pyknotid.representations.gausscode import GaussCode\n    if not isinstance(representation, GaussCode):\n        representation = GaussCode(representation)\n\n    gc = representation._gauss_code\n    if len(gc) == 0:\n        return 0\n    elif len(gc) > 1:\n        raise Exception('tried to calculate second order writhe'\n                        'for something with more than 1 component')\n\n    gc = gc[0].copy()\n\n    arrows, signs = _crossing_arrows_and_signs(\n        gc, representation.crossing_numbers)\n    \n    crossing_numbers = list(representation.crossing_numbers)\n    result = 0\n    for index, i1 in enumerate(crossing_numbers):\n        arrow1 = arrows[i1]\n        a1s, a1e = arrow1\n        for i2 in crossing_numbers[index+1:]:\n            arrow2 = arrows[i2]\n            a2s, a2e = arrow2\n\n            if ((a2s > a1e and a1s > a2s and a2e > a1s) or\n                (a2e > a1e and a1s > a2e and a2s > a1s) or\n                (a2e > a1s and a1e > a2e and a2s > a1e) or\n                (a2s > a1s and a1e > a2s and a2e > a1e)):\n            # if ((a2e > a1e and a1s > a2e and a2s > a1s) or\n            #     (a2s > a1s and a1e > a2s and a2e > a1e)):\n                result += signs[i1] * signs[i2]\n\n    return result\n\ndef arnold_2St_2Jplus(representation):\n    '''Returns J+ + 2 * St where J+ and St are Arnold's invariants of\n    plane curves.\n\n    The calculation is performed by transforming the representation\n    into a representation of an unknot by flipping crossings, then\n    calculating the second order writhe.\n\n    See 'Invariants of curves and fronts via Gauss diagrams', M\n    Polyak, Topology 37, 1998.\n\n    '''\n\n    from pyknotid.representations.gausscode import GaussCode\n    if not isinstance(representation, GaussCode):\n        representation = GaussCode(representation)\n\n    gc = representation._gauss_code\n    if len(gc) == 0:\n        return 0\n    elif len(gc) > 1:\n        raise Exception('tried to calculate arnold_2St_2Jplus'\n                        'for something with more than 1 component')\n\n    gc = gc[0].copy()\n    seen_crossings = set()\n    changed_crossings = set()\n    for row in gc:\n        if row[0] not in seen_crossings:\n            seen_crossings.add(row[0])\n            if row[1] < 0:\n                changed_crossings.add(row[0])\n                row[1] *= -1\n                row[2] *= -1\n        elif row[0] in changed_crossings:\n            row[1] *= -1\n            row[2] *= -1\n    arrows, signs = _crossing_arrows_and_signs(\n        gc, representation.crossing_numbers)\n    \n    crossing_numbers = list(representation.crossing_numbers)\n    result = 0\n    for index, i1 in enumerate(crossing_numbers):\n        arrow1 = arrows[i1]\n        a1s, a1e = arrow1\n        for i2 in crossing_numbers[index+1:]:\n            arrow2 = arrows[i2]\n            a2s, a2e = arrow2\n\n            if ((a2s > a1e and a1s > a2s and a2e > a1s) or\n                (a2e > a1e and a1s > a2e and a2s > a1s) or\n                (a2e > a1s and a1e > a2e and a2s > a1e) or\n                (a2s > a1s and a1e > a2s and a2e > a1e)):\n            # if ((a2e > a1e and a1s > a2e and a2s > a1s) or\n            #     (a2s > a1s and a1e > a2s and a2e > a1e)):\n                result += signs[i1] * signs[i2]\n\n    return result\n\ndef arnold_2St_2Jminus(representation):\n    '''Returns J- + 2 * St where J+ and St are Arnold's invariants of\n    plane curves.\n\n    See 'Invariants of curves and fronts via Gauss diagrams', M\n    Polyak, Topology 37, 1998.\n\n    '''\n    return arnold_2St_2Jplus(representation) - len(representation)\n\ndef vassiliev_degree_2(representation):\n    '''Calculates the Vassiliev invariant of degree 2 of the given\n    knot. The representation must have just one knot component,\n    this doesn't work for links.\n\n    Parameters\n    ==========\n    representation : Anything convertible to a\n                     :class:`~pyknotid.representations.gausscode.GaussCode`\n        A pyknotid representation class for the knot, or anything that\n        can automatically be converted into a GaussCode (i.e. by writing\n        :code:`GaussCode(your_object)`).\n    '''\n    ## See Polyak and Viro\n    from pyknotid.representations.gausscode import GaussCode\n    if not isinstance(representation, GaussCode):\n        representation = GaussCode(representation)\n\n    gc = representation._gauss_code\n    if len(gc) == 0:\n        return 0\n    elif len(gc) > 1:\n        raise Exception('tried to calculate v2'\n                        'for something with more than 1 component')\n\n    gc = gc[0]\n    arrows, signs = _crossing_arrows_and_signs(\n        gc, representation.crossing_numbers)\n    \n    crossing_numbers = list(representation.crossing_numbers)\n    representations_sum = 0\n    for index, i1 in enumerate(crossing_numbers):\n        arrow1 = arrows[i1]\n        a1s, a1e = arrow1\n        for i2 in crossing_numbers[index+1:]:\n            arrow2 = arrows[i2]\n            a2s, a2e = arrow2\n\n            if a2s > a1s and a2e < a1s and a1e > a2s:\n                representations_sum += signs[i1] * signs[i2]\n            elif a1s > a2s and a1e < a2s and a2e > a1s:\n                representations_sum += signs[i1] * signs[i2]\n\n    return representations_sum\n\ndef vassiliev_degree_3(representation, try_cython=True):\n    '''Calculates the Vassiliev invariant of degree 3 of the given\n    knot. if simplify:\n        representation.simplify(one=True, two=True, one_extended=True)\n\n    if len(representation._gauss_code) > 1:\n        raise Exception('tried to calculate alexander polynomial'\n                        'for something with more than 1 component')\n\n    code = representation._gauss_code[0]\n\n    if len(code) == 0:\n        return 1\n\n    mat_mat = _maxima_matrix(code, '**'))\n\n\ndef alexander_cypari(representation, quadrant='ul', verbose=False,\n                     simplify=True):\n    '''\n    Returns the Alexander polynomial of the given representation, by\n jones_polynomial(representation, var=q):\n    from pyknotid.representations.gausscode import GaussCode\n    from pyknotid.representations.planardiagram import PlanarDiagram, Crossing\n    if not isinstance(representation, (GaussCode, PlanarDiagram)):\n        representation = GaussCode(representation)\n    if isinstance(representation, GaussCode):\n        representation = PlanarDiagram(representation)\n\n    rep = representation\n\n    if isinstance(q, sym.Symbol):\n        A = sym.var('A')\n    else:\n        A = (var + 0j)**0.25\n    \n    crossings = crossings_connectedness_order(rep)\n    if len(crossings) < 2:\n        return 1\n    \n    web = []\n    crossing = crossings[0]\n    crossing = PlanarDiagram(crossing)\n    diag_1, diag_2 = kauffman_skein(crossing)\n    web.append([diag_1, A])\n    web.append([diag_2, 1.0/A])\n    replacement = -A**2 - A**-2\n\n    for crossing in crossings[1:]:\n        crossing = PlanarDiagram(crossing)\n        diag_1, diag_2 = kauffman_skein(crossing)\n        new_web = []\n\n        for term in web:\n            diag_1_product = PlanarDiagram(\n                [x for x in diag_1] +\n                [x for x in term[0]]).contract_points()\n            diag_2_product = PlanarDiagram(\n                [x for x in diag_2] +\n                [x for x in term[0]]).contract_points()\n            contracted_1 = 0\n            contracted_2 = 0\n            diag_1_new = []\n            diag_2_new = []\n\n            for point in diag_1_product:\n                if len(point.components()) == 1:\n                    contracted_1 += 1\n                else:\n                    diag_1_new.append(point)\n            for point in diag_2_product:\n                if len(point.components()) == 1:\n                    contracted_2 += 1\n                else:\n                    diag_2_new.append(point)\n\n            new_term_1 = ([sorted(diag_1_new)] +\n                          [term[1] * (replacement ** contracted_1) * A])\n            new_term_2 = ([sorted(diag_2_new)] +\n                          [term[1] * (replacement ** contracted_2) * 1.0/A])\n\n            new_web.append(new_term_1)\n            new_web.append(new_term_2)\n\n        unique_diags = list(set([tuple(x[0]) for x in new_web]))\n        contracted_web = [[list(x)] for x in unique_diags]\n\n        for term in new_web:\n            for diag in contracted_web:\n                if diag[0] == term[0]:\n                    if len(diag) == 1:\n                        diag.append(term[1])\n                    else:\n                        diag[1] += term[1]\n\n        web = contracted_web\n\n    # quick and dirty writhe calculation\n    crossings = [x for x in rep if isinstance(x, Crossing)]\n    total_writhe = 0\n    for crossing in crossings:\n        total_writhe += crossing.sign()\n        \n    jones_precursor = web[0][1]\n\n    jones_poly = (-A**3)**total_writhe * jones_precursor / replacement\n\n    if isinstance(var, sym.Symbol):\n        return sym.expand(sym.simplify(jones_poly)).subs(A, var**0.25)\n    return round(abs(jones_poly))\n\n\ndef crossings_connectedness_order(planar_diagram):\n    from pyknotid.representations.planardiagram import Crossing\n    crossings = [x for x in planar_diagram if isinstance(x, Crossing)]\n\n    inner_components = crossings[0].components()\n    crossings_remaining = crossings[1:]\n    ordered_crossings = [crossings[0]]\n\n    for i in range(len(crossings_remaining)):\n        connectedness = []\n        for crossing in crossings_remaining:\n            new_components = (len(list(set(crossing.components() + inner_components))) -\n                              len(inner_components))\n\n            connectedness.append(len(crossing.components()) - new_components)\n        most_connected_index = connectedness.index(max(connectedness))\n        inner_components += crossings_remaining[most_connected_index].components()\n        inner_components = list(set(inner_components))\n        ordered_crossings.append(crossings_remaining[most_connected_index])\n        del crossings_remaining[most_connected_index]\n\n    return ordered_crossings\n\ndef contract_points(planar_diagram):\n    '''\n    For appropriately contracting :class: `Points` in a\n    :class: `PlanarDiagram` According to the following rules:\n\n    P_a,b P_b,c -> P_a,c\n    P_a,b P_a,b -> P_a,a\n    '''\n    import copy\n\n    pd_crossings = [x for x in planar_diagram if type(x) == Crossing]\n    pd_points = [x for x in planar_diagram if type(x) == Point]\n\n    pd_trimmed = PlanarDiagram()\n    for crossing in pd_crossings:\n        pd_trimmed.append(crossing)\n\n    substituted_points = []\n    for i in range(len(pd_points)):\n        if pd_points[i] not in substituted_points:\n            pd_trimmed.append(pd_points[i])\n            totally_simplified = False\n            while not totally_simplified:\n                pd_trimmed_old = copy.copy(pd_trimmed)\n                for j in range(i+1, len(pd_points)):\n                    if (len(pd_trimmed[-1].components()) > 1 and\n                            len(pd_points[j].components()) > 1 and\n                            pd_points[j] not in substituted_points):\n                        # This skips P_a,a situations\n                        if pd_trimmed[-1].components() != pd_points[j].components():\n                            # This conditions skips (P_a,b P_a,b) situations\n                            substituted = False\n                            p = 0\n                            while substituted is not True and p != 2:\n                                q = 0\n                                while substituted is not True and q != 2:\n                                    if pd_trimmed[-1].components()[p] == pd_points[j].components()[q]:\n                                        pd_trimmed.append(Point(pd_trimmed[-1].components()[(p+1) % 2],\n                                                                pd_points[j].components()[(q+1) % 2]))\n                                        del pd_trimmed[-2]\n                                        substituted = True\n                                        substituted_points.append(pd_points[j])\n                                    q += 1\n                                p += 1\n                        else:\n                            # This deals with (P_a,b P_a,b) situations\n                            pd_trimmed.append(Point(pd_trimmed[-1].components()[0],\n                                                    pd_trimmed[-1].components()[0]))\n                            del pd_trimmed[-2]\n                            substituted_points.append(pd_points[j])\n                if pd_trimmed_old == pd_trimmed:\n                    totally_simplified = True\n    return pd_trimmed\n\n\ndef _write_mathematica_script(filen, text):\n    '''\n    Write the given text (mathematica code) to the given filename.              '$(sed \\'1,/^exit/d\\' $0) ; Exit[]\"\\nexit $?\\n')\n        fileh.write(text)\n        fileh.close()\n\n\ndef _mathematica_matrix(cs, quadrant='lr', verbose=False):\n    '''\n    Turns
